Recovery time drops accordingly."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"The observation model."}]," IR-MCL (2023) replaces the likelihood field with a neural occupancy\nfield and renders synthetic scans at arbitrary poses to score particles. The filter is unchanged —\nit is still Table 8.2 — and only line 4 was replaced. That is the modularity this chapter's\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"SensorModel"}]," trait bound is claiming."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"The alternative."}]," Pose-graph localization (SLAM Toolbox's localization mode) tracks the robot by\nmatching scans into a stored graph instead of maintaining a sampled belief. It is more accurate\nwhen it works and has no story for global ambiguity as clean as a particle cloud, which is why\nproduction stacks still ship AMCL alongside it."]}],"\n"]}]
